Clause 10.2.1.2 — If Level NTC X (defined by its NID_NTC) is not already associated to an STM, the ERTMS/ETCS on-board shall

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 10.2.1.2, PDF p.7

10.2.1.2 If Level NTC X (defined by its NID_NTC) is not already associated to an STM, the ERTMS/ETCS on-board shall associate this Level NTC X to STM X as follows: a) When a level transition order to Level NTC X is accepted, the STM X shall be the STM which NID_STM is equal to NID_NTC, if the level transition order is received from a trackside constituent with ETCS system version strictly lower than 2.0 or if the look-up table does not contain the NID_NTC value of Level NTC X. Otherwise the STM X shall be the STM having the highest priority among the available STMs linked to the NID_NTC value in the look-up table. If there is no available STM linked to this NID_NTC value, the STM X shall be the STM having the highest priority among the STMs linked to this NID_NTC value. 4 1 no STM in state HS no STM in state HS / STM(1) associated to NTC Y 2 STM(2) associated to NTC Y STM(3) associated to Continuation at NTC Z in state HS / 3 5c STM(2) associated to NTC Y STM(3) in state HS and associated to NTC Z and NTC Y

Clause 10.5.1.1 — The STM Control Function shall send the ETCS status 10.5.1.1 The STM Control Function shall send the ETCS status

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 10.5.1.1, PDF p.14

10.5.1.1 The STM Control Function shall send the ETCS status 10.5.1.1 The STM Control Function shall send the ETCS status data consisting of the current ETCS mode and level (defined data consisting of the current ETCS mode and level (defined in [1]): in [1]): a) To all connected STMs whenever the ETCS mode or level a) To all connected STMs whenever the ETCS mode or level changes. changes. b) To any STM when the connection to the STM Control Function b) To any STM when the connection to the STM Control Function is established. is established. 0 0 SRS 5.4.3.1 S10: The ERTMS/ETCS on-board equipment shall offer the possibility to the driver to select SH, NL, or to select Train Data Entry SRS E11: driver selects Train Data Entry (SEE Data ENTRY procedure FID10) 0 DataEntry / Specific STM Data entry finished SRS E20: Driver selects Start AND E30: driver ack

Clause 10.3.3.4 — The STM Control Function shall apply the emergency brake when the level is NTC X

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 10.3.3.4, PDF p.14

10.3.3.4 The STM Control Function shall apply the emergency brake when the level is NTC X and the mode is SN and STM X is known as installed on-board but not available.

Clause 10.3.3.8 — The STM Control Function shall inform the driver that the STM X is not available

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 10.3.3.8, PDF p.14

10.3.3.8 The STM Control Function shall inform the driver that the STM X is not available while all of the following conditions are fulfilled -- the level is NTC X, - and (the mode is SN) or (the mode is NL and has been so for at least 5s), - and STM X is known as installed on-board but not available, - and STM X is not known to be isolated through the corresponding input on the Train Interface. 0 STM(2) reports CS STM(2) in state CS ETCS Level NTC X Version 3.1.0 2. 3.8 Test case 5b.1 TEST CASE HEADER Test case identification Level Transition STM to STM 5b.0.1.0.2.0.3.0 Check of handling of the STM max speed and STM system speed / distance within the ERTMS/ETCS on-board (STM Control Function) during a level transition STM to STM. The STM is finally kept as failed. 3.12 Test case 5c.1 TEST CASE HEADER Test case identification Level Transition STM to STM 5c.1.0.1.1.1.1.0.1.0 Check the behaviour of the ERTMS/ETCS on-board and the STM at a level transition border (announced by balise group), when the ERTMS/ETCS on-board is in the ETCS technical mode SN (STM national): ERTMS/ETCS on-board orders the old STM to state CS. When the ERTMS/ETCS on-board receives the CS state from the old STM, it orders the new STM to state DA and supervises the “STM max speed”. The STM follows the state transition order to state DA in due time. The stored ‘STM max speed’ at the border is 60km/h << actual speed of 100 km/h ERTMS/ETCS on-board Subset-035: 8.7.1.2, 10.3.2.2 (DA->CS, HS->DA); 10.3.2.4 (A4b, A9); 10.3.2.6; 10.3.3.6 c); 10.3.2.7 (A4b); 10.5.1.1 a); 10.12.2.1, 13.2.1.2 requirements tested Version 3.1.0 STM requirements tested Subset-035: 9.2.1.1 (DA->CS, HS->DA); 9.2.1.2 (4b, 9); 9.3.1.4 c) Packets transmitted via STM-5, STM-14, STM-15, STM-16 FFFIS STM ERTMS/ETCS on-board - STM(1) is associated to NTC X configuration - STM(2) is associated to NTC Y Transition has been acknowledged by the driver before the transition border Comments and STMs involved: constraints STM(1): old STM (in DA at start of test case) for active level at start of transition STM(2): new STM (in HS at start of test case) for announced level Test Case is designed for testing when STM(2) in HS has all connections (DMI, TIU, BIU and JD) established and STM(1) ordered in CS state closes all the connections except STM Control Function connection.. ERTMS/ETCS on-board Subset-035: 8.7.1.2, 10.3.2.2 (DA->CS, HS->DA); 10.3.2.4 (A4b, A9); 10.3.2.6; 10.3.2.7 (A4b), 10.5.1.1 a) requirements tested Version 3.1.0 STM requirements tested Subset-035: 9.2.1.1 (DA->CS, HS->DA); 9.2.1.2 (4b, 9); 9.3.1.4 c) Packets transmitted via STM-5, STM-14, STM-15 FFFIS STM ERTMS/ETCS on-board - STM(1) is associated to NTC X configuration - STM(2) is associated to NTC Y Comments and constraints STMs involved: STM(1): old STM (in DA at start of test case) for active level at start of transition STM(2): new STM (in HS at start of test case) for announced level Test Case is designed for testing when STM in HS has the connections (TIU, BIU and JD) established and STM ordered in CS state closes all the connections except STM Control Function connection. Clause 3.14 — Test case 5c.3

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 3.14, PDF p.111

3.14 Test case 5c.3 TEST CASE HEADER Test case identification Level Transition STM to STM 5c.1.0.1.1.1.1.0.3.0 Check the behaviour of the ERTMS/ETCS on-board and the STM at a first level transition border (transmitted by balise group) with no announcement of the transition (i.e. new STM in CS) to Level NTC Y and a second level transition border (transmitted by balise group) with no announcement of the transition (i.e. new STM in CS) back to Level NTC X. The ERTMS/ETCS on-board is in the ETCS technical mode SN (STM national) and the STM follows the state transition order in due time. ERTMS/ETCS on-board Subset-035: 8.7.1.2; 10.2.1.2 a); 10.2.1.3; 10.3.2.2 (DA->CS, CS->DA); 10.3.2.4 (A4b, A9); 10.3.2.6; 10.3.2.7 (A4b), 10.5.1.1 a); 13.2.1.2 requirements tested STM requirements tested Subset-035: 9.2.1.1 (DA->CS, CS->DA); 9.2.1.2 (4b, 9); 9.3.1.4 c) Packets transmitted via STM-5, STM-14, STM-15 FFFIS STM ERTMS/ETCS on-board - STM(1) is associated to NTC X and mapped to NTC Y by the look-up table (lower priority) configuration - STM(2) is mapped to NTC X and NTC Y by the look-up table (higher priority) Comments and With this kind of ERTMS/ETCS on-board configuration this TC covers both test of: constraints - the association "freezing" (STM(1) remains in DA despite STM(2) is available & mapped to the current Level NTC X with an higher priority) - and the association "de-freezing" (STM(1) is not associated again to Level NTC Y or Level NTC X at the level transitions) Version 3.1.0 STMs involved: STM(1): STM in DA at start of test case for active level at start of transition STM(2): STM in CS at start of test case Test Case is designed for testing when STM(2) in CS has all connections (DMI, TIU, BIU and JD) established and STM(1) ordered in CS state closes all the connections except STM Control Function connection. To have at the beginning of the TC STM(1) in DA and STM(2) in CS in Level NTC X despite STM(2) handles NTC X with an higher priority, STM(2) should not be available (e.g. by having a non-final disconnection with STM(2)) when the level NTC X is selected after a first Start of Mission done in another level. Clause 3.15 — Test case 5c.4

Clause 3.29 — Test case 5d.7

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 3.29, PDF p.232

3.29 Test case 5d.7 TEST CASE HEADER Version 3.1.0 Test case identification Level Transition STM to STM 5d.0.1.2.3.0.1 Check the behaviour of the ERTMS/ETCS on-board, when STM(1) has stopped the train due to an national trip situation and the driver manually selects a level transition from NTC X to NTC Y on the DMI, while the national trip procedure of STM(1) is still active. The ERTMS/ETCS on-board orders the STM(1) from DA to CS state. STM(1) does not report the “National Trip Procedure” in due time and is ordered to FA state. Afterwards the ERTMS/ETCS on-board orders STM(2) from CS to DA state and STM(2) reports the new state in due time. ERTMS/ETCS on-board Subset-035: 10.2.1.2 c); 10.3.2.2 (DA->CS, DA->FA, CS->DA); 10.3.2.4 (B4b, F16, A9); 10.3.2.6; 10.3.2.7 (B4b); 10.3.3.3, 10.5.1.1 a); 10.13.1.1; requirements tested 10.14.1.1, 13.2.1.2 STM requirements tested Subset-035: None Packets transmitted via STM-5; STM-14, STM-15 FFFIS STM ERTMS/ETCS on-board - STM(1) associated to NTC X configuration - STM(2) is mapped to NTC Y by the look-up table Comments and STMs involved: constraints STM(1): old STM (in DA at start of test case) for active level at start of transition STM(2): new STM (in CS at start of test case) for announced level The ERTMS/ETCS on-board Test Case is designed for testing ERTMS/ETCS on-board when STM ordered in CS closes all the connections except STM Control Function connection. The STM test case is designed for both STMs that, when ordered in CS, close all the connections except STM Control Function connection or keep all connections active. Clause 3.30 — Test case 5e.1

Citation: SUBSET-074-2 v3.1.0, clause 3.30, PDF p.239

3.30 Test case 5e.1 TEST CASE HEADER Test case Identification Level Transition STM to STM 5e.0.1.0.0.0 The driver selects ETCS Level = NTC x (in look-up table, STM (1) has higher priority than STM (2) both configured for NTC X) but STM (1) has failed, the ETCS mode is SB. The driver selects Train Data Entry, test continues in another TC (from FID 10), driver validates train data and this other TC ends. The driver then selects Start and acknowledges SN mode. The STM (2) is still in CO and ETCS displays “STM (2) not available” to the driver. When the STM (2) requests CS mode, it is ordered to CS and the message “STM (2) not available” is removed.
